- **scrumia-design-sync**: Syncs the local design system with a claude.ai/design project through the DesignSync tool — component by component, never as a wholesale replace. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-design/skills/scrumia-design-sync))
- **scrumia-design-system**: The ScrumIA design-system reference — where identity, tokens and components live, and how to consult them before writing an interface. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-design/skills/scrumia-design-system))
- **scrumia-effect**: The typed-effect discipline for an agent — describe before execute, failure as a value, the effect boundary, and which approach to reach for (Result, Either, IO/suspend, or effect.website). Library-agnostic on the pattern; cites effect.website as the reference implementation rather than wrapping it. Load before writing or reviewing code that produces, transforms, or consumes a side effect in an app where this module is plugged in. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-effect/skills/scrumia-effect))
- **scrumia-extend**: The ScrumIA extension protocol — how a main skill opens a register, how any module contributes directives to it as pure data, and how scrumia-extends renders the table an agent picks from. Load it before writing or changing a module's extends.json, registers.json or dependencies.jsonl, or before adding an extension point to a skill. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-core/skills/scrumia-extend))
- **scrumia-feature**: Creates, updates or audits a ScrumIA feature in features/. - **scrumia-functional-programming**: The ScrumIA Functional Programming paradigm — purity, total functions, referential transparency, immutability, composition over inheritance, and effect discipline, all in language-neutral terms so the same wording holds in Kotlin, JavaScript, Rust and any language that supports them. Load it before writing code in an app that extends this module. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-functional-programming/skills/scrumia-functional-programming))
- **scrumia-gradle**: The ScrumIA Gradle reference — Kotlin DSL over Groovy, the version catalog as the one place versions live, the shape of a convention plugin (build-logic composite, precompiled script plugin), lazy task configuration, the build and configuration caches, composite builds for local siblings, pluginManagement, and documentation tasks wired into the lifecycle. Stated in terms of Gradle, not Kotlin Multiplatform. Load it before writing or reviewing a Gradle build script. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-gradle/skills/scrumia-gradle))
- **scrumia-gradle-audit**: Audits an existing Gradle project against the eight rules this module ships — Kotlin DSL over Groovy, version catalog usage, convention plugin shape, lazy task configuration, the build and configuration caches, composite builds for local siblings, pluginManagement, and documentation tasks wired into the lifecycle. Use it before adopting the module on an existing codebase, when a build shape change ships, or to verify a project held to the rules really is. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-gradle/skills/scrumia-gradle-audit))
- **scrumia-init**: Installs or verifies a project's ScrumIA composition — creates .scrumia/config.yaml, the specs tree, and writes into CLAUDE.md which modules are plugged in and where. - **scrumia-kotlin**: The ScrumIA Kotlin reference — naming and scope functions, null-safety and platform types, coroutines and Flow, data/sealed/value classes, object vs companion vs top-level, and visibility modifiers. - **scvelo**: scVelo is the leading Python package for RNA velocity analysis in single-cell RNA-seq data. It infers cell state transitions by modeling the kinetics of mRNA splicing — using the ratio of unspliced (pre-mRNA) to spliced (mature mRNA) abundances to determine whether a gene is being upregulated or downregulated in each cell. This allows reconstruction of developmental trajectories and identification ([ComeOnOliver/skillshub](https://github.com/ComeOnOliver/skillshub/tree/HEAD/skills/K-Dense-AI/claude-scientific-skills/scvelo) · ★63)
- **scvelo**: RNA velocity analysis with scVelo. Estimate cell state transitions from unspliced/spliced mRNA dynamics, infer trajectory directions, compute latent time, and identify driver genes in single-cell RNA-seq data. Complements Scanpy/scVI-tools for trajectory inference. Use when you need help with scvelo. ([anubhavg-icpl/vibe](https://github.com/anubhavg-icpl/vibe/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-scvelo) · ★9)
- **scvelo**: RNA velocity analysis with scVelo. - **scvelo**: Performs RNA velocity analysis with scVelo from spliced and unspliced single-cell RNA counts.
